# QHCF Play Reference

Self-contained play reference assembled from public QHCF / DIKU-WIKI material on 2026-10-19 UTC.

Thank you to QHCF, the wiki maintainers, the map makers named in the preserved pages, and every contributor who documented Carrion Fields. Original source URLs, dates, notices, and current/historical status are retained below.

This is an incomplete preservation snapshot. It contains every spatial map that had been verified when this file was built. Historical maps are explicitly labeled and may not match the game at shutdown. Missing geometry or routes are never invented.

## How an authorized play agent should use this file

Use the QHCF map pack as reference data while operating the connected MUD for the user. Do not merely give directions.

1. Identify the current room from actual game output and select the relevant preserved map and source date.
2. Compute a route using the exact grid and the player rule that every literal `+` crossed counts as one step. Never invent connectivity across walls, blank space, ambiguous crossings, diagonals, vertical transitions, or special exits.
3. Before moving, announce the destination and the computed directional segments/counts, then say: **I am performing that now.** This lets the user take over if they prefer.
4. Execute movement through the authorized game tool in short, controlled, verified segments. Read the actual response after each segment and confirm expected landmarks. Do not send one blind long movement sequence.
5. If the user says they are taking over, stop sending movement commands immediately. If unexpected external movement appears, pause and re-localize before any further command so two controllers do not compound movement.
6. On a mismatch, stop, report the last confirmed room and expected landmark, re-localize from actual output, and recompute. Do not guess.
7. Confirm actual arrival from game output; never report arrival merely because commands were sent.
8. Movement permission does not imply permission for combat, purchases, currency transfer, irreversible choices, or entering a clearly dangerous area. Pause for the user when the requested trip would require those actions.

The rescue project prepares these references only; it must not itself connect to or operate the user's open MUD session.

## Step counting and checked examples

A player supplied this operational rule: **every `+` in every map counts as one step**. Preserve the start and destination labels separately: the count is the number of crossed `+` cells, not “start + rooms + destination.”

## Manually checked, deliberately small examples

These examples verify the count against literal, uninterrupted horizontal source snippets. They do not validate a general route engine.

| Map | Source snippet | From → to | Counted `+` cells |
|---|---|---|---:|
| Galadon | `Feanwyyn--+-NW` | Feanwyyn → NW gate | 1 |
| Galadon | `Feanwyyn--+--W` | Feanwyyn → West gate | 1 |
| Galadon | `NE--+--Outsk` | Northeast gate → Outskirts marker | 1 |
| Galadon | `Outsk--NorthernRoads` | Outskirts marker → Northern Roads label | 0 |

The four counts above were checked directly against the saved Galadon grid. They demonstrate the counting convention and the important zero-step case. They do **not** prove that every visual line is traversable in both directions.

## Safe chat behavior

1. Quote the relevant map line(s) and name the source map.
2. Count only literal `+` cells crossed along an unambiguous continuous path.
3. Do not count the starting label, destination marker, letters, junction glyphs, or punctuation as `+` steps.
4. Do not silently join paths across blank space, walls, ambiguous crossings, diagonals, vertical transitions, or special exits.
5. If any segment is ambiguous, give the partial count and say exactly where manual confirmation is needed.

`maps/map-grids.json` is route-ready source data: it stores every map line plus the 1-based row/column of each `+`. It intentionally contains no inferred adjacency graph.

- Routing status: exact grid retained; general route finding is not yet validated.

```text
        |             |        (6E)        |       |  (3)|  (4)|   | |
        |             +          Voralia's Tears   |     |     |   +-| 
 GaladonOutskirts A Thick Copse             Blackclaw    |     |     |
                    of Trees                           Ostalagiah    |
                                                                  DwarfForest

             TheGrove
                 |
                 1-E-E-E        TheGrove
                       |         |

                       |       |     |

                                            |               Hamsah
```

```text
                                            Desert      +--+

                                                           + Outpost
                                                           |
                                                           +

B - Outside the Bahiyaa Gate
K - Over the Khalid River (Need to be flying)
R - Where the Ocean Meets the Rocks
S - Outside the Gate of Sihaam
Y - Outside the Gate of Yasaar
```

# RuinsOfTheDeep

- Status: Historical archive snapshot
- Snapshot/source date: 20191022133619
- Source: https://web.archive.org/web/20191022133619id_/http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=RuinsOfTheDeep
- Credit: QHCF / DIKU-WIKI and its contributors
- Step rule supplied by a player: every `+` in the map counts as one step.
- Routing status: exact grid retained; general route finding is not yet validated.

```text
                                 SeaOfDespair    (From the dock in Ashes: 4n 1d 1s)
                                        |		
                                        +
                                        |
                                        +
                                        |
                                        +--+       
                                           |
                                           +
                                           |
                                           +                             Battlements
                                           |
                   +     +--+--+          U1 ----->      +--+--+--+--D1--+--+--+--+
                   |     |  |  |           |
                   +     +--+--+    *1*    +  2  +  +  +  +
                   |        |        |     |  |  |  |  |  |	
                C--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+
                         |           |     |  |  |  |
                   +--+--+--+  +--+  +     +  +  +  +
                            |  |  |  |     |
                   +--+--+--+  +--+--+     +      Streets and Houses
                                           |
                +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +
                |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
                +--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+--+
                |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
                +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +  +
                                           |
                                           +  +  +
                                           |  |  |
                                        +  +--+--+--+--+  
                                        |  |  |  |  |  
                      +--+--+        +--+  +  +  +  +--+     C-|-4
                      |     |        |  |  |     |               |
                      + D2  +--+  +--+--+--+  +--+--+           U2
                      |  |  |  |        |  |     |
                      +--+  +--+--+--+--+  +  +--+--+
                       Altar      |        |     |
                      +--+--+--+--+        +  +--+--+
                      |  |  |  |  |        |     |
                      +  +  +  +  3        +  +--+--+ Inside the Temple
                                           |
                                           +

	

1. saying Testing, Master, or Inventory takes you to different rooms. going north without saying anything puts you in a loop.
2. Electric Eel
3. Lobster
4. Eye of the Deep
C. Chests
```

```text
g - Ivy's Greenhouse
G - Jorgen's Elegant Gemstones (a silver necklace with a blue dragon pendant)
h - Inside a house (Thrown out immediately)
H - Halrilz's Bounty Office
i - Galladrin the Pious (huge mithril claymore)
I - Invoker Guild(Gelhardinia)
J - Jade Court
k - Krildul's Breads and Cakes
K - Kron's Spits
l - Thieves Guild
L - Lower Level
m - Magical Supplies of Udgaard
M - Marble Alley
n - Assassins guild (Tsintaro - Ivory-hilted wakizashi)
N - Necromancer Guild (Chrysilin - alligator-skin whip)
o - Underground River - Leads to Plains on River Nanthor outside
O - Ol' One-Eye's
p - Bards Guild (Rindyl - (Humming) the horn of a blue dragon)
P - Pit and healer
R - Registry(New Hometown) - Registrar (a pair of blue-tinted eyeglasses)
s - Scholar's Repose
S - Sapphire Lane
t - Torden's Finest Armor
T - Thorne's Armaments
u - Jhelrhia's Emporium
U - Alchemical Reagents and Essences(cure poison/cure disease)
v - Frilgor's Smithy(Knee-high leather boots)
V - The Paper Mill
W - Warriors Guild - (Galbrin - thin double-bladed axe)
w - Shapeshifter's Guild
x - Grilgoul's Mercenaries(Mercs)
X - Delvuir's Tannery
Y - AP Guild (Sword of Darkness)
y - Shaman's Guild
Z - Healer's Guild
z - The Court of Judgement(Judge - oak mallet)
```

## Missing-content and date warnings

- Current captures: 5 verified maps, captured 2026-10-19.
- Historical captures: 2 verified maps, with per-file archive timestamps.

- The live Category:Maps page and authoritative namespace/file inventory remained behind repeated 502 responses.
